Q1. A, B and C alone take 10, 20 and 18 days. For the first 3 days the initial phase runs, then B and C join. Find the total time.
A. 12.41 days
B. 7.41 days
C. 6 2/5 days
D. 8.01 days
Answer: 6 2/5 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_multi_phase_join_leave
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
\sum r_it_i=W
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=18\times 3
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=3+\frac{126}{37}=6.41
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]
Answer: 6 2/5 days

---

Q2. Pipe A fills a tank in 12 hours. Pipes A and B together fill it in 6 hours. Find the time taken by B alone.
A. 15 hours
B. 12 hours
C. 7 hours
D. 9 hours
Answer: 12 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_find_pipe_rate_from_net_time
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{6}-\frac{1}{12}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
B=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{AT}{A-T}
\]
Answer: 12 hours

---

Q3. A worker does 15 units per day for 5 days, then slows to 12 units per day. Total work is 180 units. Find total time.
A. 13 3/4 days
B. 19 3/4 days
C. 12 3/4 days
D. 11 days
Answer: 13 3/4 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_productivity_decay
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-r_1t_1}{r_2}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
T=5+\frac{180-15\times 5}{12}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=13.75
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+t_2
\]
Answer: 13 3/4 days

---

Q4. One team completes 15 units per day while another undoes 3 units per day. For 180 units, find the net completion time.
A. 15 days
B. 20 days
C. 11 1/4 days
D. 12 days
Answer: 15 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_negative_positive_mixed_work
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_+-r_-}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
T=\frac{180}{15-3}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_n}
\]
Answer: 15 days

---

Q5. A and B alternate turns, and work stops as soon as 60 units are completed. Their turn outputs are 8 and 5 units. Find the time.
A. 5 days
B. 9 days
C. 8 days
D. 10 4/5 days
Answer: 9 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_terminal_overshoot_cycle
Explanation:
Find the work completed in one full cycle.
\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]
Count full cycles before the last turn.
\[
W_{cycle}=8+5
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=9
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 9 days

---

Q6. A contract wage of ₹7200 is divided in proportion to contributions 8:12. Find the required share.
A. ₹3600
B. ₹2886
C. ₹2881
D. ₹2880
Answer: ₹2880
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_wage_partial_time
Explanation:
Convert each person's work into contribution units.
\[
Share=\frac{c_i}{\sum c}\times W
\]
Use contribution ratio for the share.
\[
Share=\frac{8}{20}\times 7200
\]
Apply the wage or contract amount.
\[
Share=2880
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
Share=contribution\ ratio\times wage
\]
Answer: ₹2880

---

Q7. A completes \(\frac{4}{9}\) of a work in 4 days. Find the time needed for the whole work.
A. 9 days
B. 13 days
C. 1.78 days
D. 36 days
Answer: 9 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_partial_completion_then_team
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
T=d\times \frac{q}{p}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
T=4\times \frac{9}{4}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=9
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=4\div \frac{4}{9}=9
\]
Answer: 9 days

W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]

\[
W_{cycle}=10+4
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=12.6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 12 3/5 days

Q9. A group consumes food at 30 adult-equivalent units per day. The stock is 720 units. Find how many days it will last.
A. 24 days
B. 12 days
C. 22 days
D. 30 days
Answer: 24 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_resource_rate_equivalence
Explanation:
Convert the stock into consumption units.
\[
D=\frac{S}{R}
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
D=\frac{720}{30}
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=\frac{S}{R}
\]
Answer: 24 days

---

Q10. Food for 20 people lasts 30 days. How many days will it last for 40 people?
A. 15 days
B. 22 1/2 days
C. 13 days
D. 11 days
Answer: 15 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_food_resource_basic
Explanation:
Convert the stock into consumption units.
\[
M_1D_1=M_2D_2
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
20\times 30=40\times D_2
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D_2=15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D_2=\frac{20\times 30}{40}
\]
Answer: 15 days

---

Q11. A mixed team has worker counts 6, 8 and relative efficiencies 4, 3. Find the time for 360 units of work.
A. 2 1/2 days
B. 5 1/2 days
C. 7 1/2 days
D. 5.63 days
Answer: 7 1/2 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_group_conversion_and_or
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
T=\frac{W}{\sum ne}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
T=\frac{360}{6\times 4+8\times 3}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=7.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=W/R
\]
Answer: 7 1/2 days

Q12. A+B finish in 10 days, B+C in 12 days and C+A in 15 days. Find A alone's time.
A. 24 days
B. 36 days
C. 20 days
D. 22 days
Answer: 24 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_pairwise_ab_bc_ac
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
2(A+B+C)=(A+B)+(B+C)+(C+A)
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
AB=10,\ BC=12,\ CA=15
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T_A=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
A=\frac{AB+AC-BC}{2}
\]
Answer: 24 days

---

Q13. Three machines produce 12, 15, 18 units per hour. They run together for 8 hours. Find total output.
A. 355 units
B. 450 units
C. 359 units
D. 360 units
Answer: 360 units
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_machine_breakdown_midway
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
O=\sum r_it_i
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
O=12\times 8+15\times 8+18\times 8
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
O=360
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
O=(r_1+r_2+r_3)t
\]
Answer: 360 units

---

Q14. Two filling pipes take 12 hours and 18 hours. An emptying pipe can empty the tank in 36 hours. Find the net filling time.
A. 7 hours
B. 9 hours
C. 6 3/4 hours
D. 4 hours
Answer: 9 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_two_fillers_one_empty
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
T=\frac{W_l}{r_f-r_e}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
r=1/12+1/18-1/36
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
T=9
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W_l}{r_n}
\]
Answer: 9 hours

---

Q15. A job has 180 units. 8 workers finish 80 units, and 10 days are left. Find the extra workers required.
A. 0.5 workers
B. 3 workers
C. 6 workers
D. 2 workers
Answer: 2 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_contract_deadline_extra_workers
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-N_c
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{100}{1\times 10}\right\rceil-8
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=2
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 2 workers

---

Q16. A tank is filled for one hour at 12 units/hour and then drained for one hour at 5 units/hour repeatedly. Capacity is 60 units. Find completion time.
A. 20.42 hours
B. 16 1/3 hours
C. 15 1/3 hours
D. 11 1/3 hours
Answer: 16 1/3 hours
Difficulty: Hard
Family: pc_alternating_fill_empty
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
W_{cycle}=12-5
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
60\ units,\ cycle=7
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
T=16\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=C_f+t_f
\]
Answer: 16 1/3 hours

\]

\]
Answer: 18.86 days

Q18. A and B are paid ₹300 and ₹500 for equal time. Find the ratio of their work rates.
A. 800:500
B. 3:5
C. 30:25
D. 500:300
Answer: 3:5
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_efficiency_from_wages
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
W_A:W_B=E_A:E_B
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
E_A:E_B=300:500
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
E_A:E_B=3:5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
E=3:5
\]
Answer: 3:5

Q21. A and B begin a job together. After 2 days A leaves. If A and B alone take 10 and 30 days, find when the job ends.
A. 36 days
B. 28 days
C. 23 days
D. 24 days
Answer: 24 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_forward_leave
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W=(r_A+r_B)t_1+r_Bt_2
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=(12+4)\times 2
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=2+\frac{88}{4}=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=2+\frac{W-W_1}{B}
\]
Answer: 24 days

Q22. Pipe A can fill a tank in 12 hours and pipe B in 18 hours. A runs only for the first 2 hours while B continues. Find total filling time.

Answer: 15 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_pipe_closed_before_completion

\[
W_1=(r_A+r_B)t_1,\quad T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_B}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=(3+2)\times 2
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=2+\frac{26}{2}=15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_B}
\]
Answer: 15 hours

Q23. Pipe A fills a tank in 12 hours. It is opened first, and pipe B, which fills it in 18 hours, is opened 2 hours later. Find the total time.
A. 3 hours
B. 10 hours
C. 8 hours
D. 7 hours
Answer: 8 hours
Difficulty: Hard
Family: pc_multiple_pipes_timing
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W_1=r_At_1,\quad T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=3\times 2
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=2+\frac{30}{3+2}=8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Answer: 8 hours

Q24. A job needs 15 men for 16 days at 6 hours per day. Find the men required for 12 days at 8 hours per day.
A. 18 workers
B. 11 workers
C. 15 workers
D. 20 workers
Answer: 15 workers
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_man_days_hours_basic
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
15\times 16\times 6=M_2\times 12\times 8
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
M_2=15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{15\times 16\times 6}{12\times 8}=15
\]
Answer: 15 workers

Q27. A team completes a work in 10 days. Known worker times are 20 and 40 days. Find the unknown worker's time.

B. 42 days

D. 45 days

Family: tw_unknown_worker_from_team

\[
r_X=r_{team}-\sum r_i
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
r_X=\frac{1}{10}-\frac{1}{20}-\frac{1}{40}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T_X=40
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T_X=1/r_X
\]
Answer: 40 days

Q30. A completes a job in 12 days, while B completes it in 24 days. Find the time required when both work together.
A. 12 days
B. 8 days
C. 18 days
D. 36 days
Answer: 8 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(12,24)=24
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=24/12=2,\quad B=24/24=1
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{24}{2+1}=8
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{12\times 24}{12+24}=8
\]
Answer: 8 days

---

Q31. If a job takes 10 days and 4 days have already been worked, what part is still unfinished?

Family: tw_remaining_work_fraction

\[
R=1-\frac{4}{10}
\]

\[
R=\frac{6}{10}=3/5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
R=\frac{10-4}{10}=3/5
\]
Answer: 3/5

Q36. A:B efficiency is 3:4. If both together complete a job in 12 days, how long will A alone take?

\]

B. 180 pages
C. 229 pages
D. 223 pages
Answer: 225 pages
Difficulty: Medium

Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
O=\sum r_it_i
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
O=12\times 5+15\times 5+18\times 5
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
O=225
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
O=(r_1+r_2+r_3)t
\]
Answer: 225 pages

Q39. 15 men working 6 hours a day finish a work in 16 days. How many men are needed to finish it in 12 days working 8 hours a day?
A. 11 workers
B. 18 workers
C. 15 workers
D. 20 workers
Answer: 15 workers
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_road_construction_work
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
15\times 16\times 6=M_2\times 12\times 8
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
M_2=15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{15\times 16\times 6}{12\times 8}=15
\]
Answer: 15 workers

Q40. A completes a job in 20 days, while B completes it in 26 days. Find the time required when both work together.
A. 20 days
B. 23 days
C. 11.3 days
D. 46 days
Answer: 11.3 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(20,26)=260
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=260/20=13,\quad B=260/26=10
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{260}{13+10}=11.3
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{20\times 26}{20+26}=11.3
\]
Answer: 11.3 days

\]

\]
Answer: 18 hours

\]

\]

\[
T=9
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[

\]
Answer: 9 hours

\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[

\]
Answer: 2:1

Q77. A starts a job alone and works for 4 days. A and B then finish it together. A alone takes 8 days and B alone takes 12 days. Find the total time.
A. 5 2/5 days
B. 12 2/5 days
C. 6 2/5 days
D. 5.12 days
Answer: 6 2/5 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_delayed_join
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W_1=r_At_1,\ T_2=\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=9\times 4,\quad R=72-36
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=4+\frac{36}{9+6}=6.4
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=4+\frac{72-9\times 4}{9+6}
\]
Answer: 6 2/5 days

\]

\[

\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:

Q126. A can finish a work in 12 days and B in 18 days. In how many days can they finish it together?

B. 30 days

Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:

\[

\]

\[

\]

\[

\]

\[
T=\frac{12\times 18}{12+18}=7.2
\]
Answer: 7 1/5 days

D. 9 2/5 days
Answer: 9 2/5 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_terminal_overshoot_cycle
Explanation:

\]

\[

\]

\[
T=9.4
\]

\[

\]
Answer: 9 2/5 days

\]

A. 16 2/3 days
B. 18 2/3 days
C. 13 1/3 days
D. 22 2/3 days
Answer: 16 2/3 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_worker_equivalence_men_women_children
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
T=\frac{W}{\sum ne}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
T=\frac{300}{2\times 4+3\times 2+4\times 1}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=16\frac{2}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=W/R
\]
Answer: 16 2/3 days

Q191. A and B together complete a work in 6 days. A alone takes 12 days. Find the time taken by B alone.
A. 6 days
B. 9 days
C. 18 days
D. 12 days
Answer: 12 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_individual_from_combined
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{6}-\frac{1}{12}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
B=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{6\times 12}{12-6}=12
\]
Answer: 12 days

Q193. 120 pages are printed per hour for 8 hours, but only 90% are accepted. Find the accepted pages.
A. 864 pages
B. 691.2 pages
C. 863 pages
D. 870 pages
Answer: 864 pages
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_work_quality_rejection
Explanation:
Convert each person's work into contribution units.
\[
O_a=O\times \frac{p}{100}
\]
Use contribution ratio for the share.
\[
O_a=120\times 8\times \frac{90}{100}
\]
Apply the wage or contract amount.
\[
O_a=864
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
O_a=gross\times p/100
\]
Answer: 864 pages

Q194. A two-hour work cycle gives 10 units in the first hour and 6 units in the second. Find the total hours for 60 units.
A. 7 1/3 hours
B. 9.17 hours
C. 2 1/3 hours
D. 6 1/3 hours
Answer: 7 1/3 hours
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_alternating_hours
Explanation:
Find the work completed in one full cycle.
\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]
Count full cycles before the last turn.
\[
W_{cycle}=10+6
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=7\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 7 1/3 hours

Q195. Typists A, B and C type at 12, 15, 18 pages per hour. If all work for 6 hours, find the output.

\[
O=12\times 6+15\times 6+18\times 6
\]

\[
O=270
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
O=(r_1+r_2+r_3)t
\]
Answer: 270 pages

Q201. A, B and C alone take 12, 18 and 15 days. For the first 4 days the initial phase runs, then B is replaced by C. Find the total time.
A. 5.22 days
B. 3.96 days
C. 11.96 days
D. 6.96 days
Answer: 6.96 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_replacement_worker
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
r_1t_1+r_2t_2=W
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=25\times 4
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=4+\frac{80}{27}=6.96
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]
Answer: 6.96 days

Q204. Two workers have efficiencies 5:7. Their combined time is 8 days. Find A's individual time.
A. 5.71 days
B. 19 1/5 days
C. 11 1/5 days
D. 20 days
Answer: 19 1/5 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_efficiency_ratio_scaling
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
W=(a+b)T
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
W=(5+7)\times 8
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T_A=\frac{96}{5}=19.2
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T_A=\frac{(a+b)T}{a}=\frac{(5+7)8}{5}=19.2
\]
Answer: 19 1/5 days

Q205. A and B need 16.36 active days together. If there is a 2-day pause, find the total calendar duration.

C. 19.36 days
D. 18.36 days
Answer: 18.36 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_interrupted_work
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
T_{calendar}=T_{active}+T_{idle}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
T_{active}=\frac{180}{6+5}
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=T_{active}+2=18.36
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_a+t_i
\]
Answer: 18.36 days

Q206. 12 workers complete a job in 10 days by working 8 hours daily. For 6 hours daily and 16 days, find the required workers.
A. 13 workers
B. 19 workers
C. 10 workers
D. 12 workers
Answer: 10 workers
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_road_construction_work
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
M_1D_1H_1=M_2D_2H_2
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
12\times 10\times 8=M_2\times 16\times 6
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
M_2=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
M_2=\frac{12\times 10\times 8}{16\times 6}=10
\]
Answer: 10 workers

Q207. A can finish a work in 6 days and B in 15 days. In how many days can they finish it together?
A. 4.29 days
B. 10 1/2 days
C. 21 days
D. 6 days
Answer: 4.29 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_combined_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(6,15)=30
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=30/6=5,\quad B=30/15=2
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{30}{5+2}=4.29
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{6\times 15}{6+15}=4.29
\]
Answer: 4.29 days

Q208. Normally a tank fills in 15 hours, but with leakage it takes 21 hours. Find the leak's emptying time.
A. 49 1/2 hours
B. 39.37 hours
C. 52 1/2 hours
D. 57 1/2 hours
Answer: 52 1/2 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_leak_hidden_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{F}-\frac{1}{T}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{15}-\frac{1}{21}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
L=52.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 52 1/2 hours

Q212. A can finish a work in 12 days and B in 6 days. A works alone for 2 days, then B joins. Find the total time taken.

B. 6 2/5 days

D. 5 1/3 days
Answer: 5 1/3 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_delayed_join

\[

\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[

\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=2+\frac{10}{1+2}=5\frac{1}{3}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=2+\frac{12-1\times 2}{1+2}
\]
Answer: 5 1/3 days

\]

\]
Answer: 17 workers

\]

T=\frac{6\times 20}{6+20}=4.62
\]
Answer: 4.62 days

30\times 36=40\times D_2
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D_2=27
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D_2=\frac{30\times 36}{40}
\]
Answer: 27 days

Q266. After a delay, 96 of 240 work units are done by 12 workers. To meet a 8-day deadline, find extra workers.
A. 12 workers
B. 6 workers
C. 7.5 workers
D. 4 workers
Answer: 6 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_contract_deadline_extra_workers
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-N_c
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{144}{1\times 8}\right\rceil-12
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 6 workers

Q273. Working alone, A takes 18 days and B takes 24 days for the same work. Find their combined time.
A. 18 days
B. 21 days
C. 42 days
D. 10.29 days
Answer: 10.29 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_lcm_hidden_total_work
Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(18,24)=72
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=72/18=4,\quad B=72/24=3
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{72}{4+3}=10.29
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{18\times 24}{18+24}=10.29
\]
Answer: 10.29 days

Q280. A pipe fills a tank in 15 hours. With a leak, it fills in 25 hours. Find the time in which the leak alone empties the full tank.

B. 40 1/2 hours
C. 33 1/2 hours
D. 37 1/2 hours
Answer: 37 1/2 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_leak_hidden_rate
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[

\]

\[
\frac{1}{L}=\frac{1}{15}-\frac{1}{25}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
L=37.5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
L=\frac{FT}{T-F}
\]
Answer: 37 1/2 hours

Family: tw_work_quality_rejection

\]

\[

\]
Apply the wage or contract amount.
\[
O_a=510
\]

Q313. Pipe A fills a tank in 20 hours. Pipes A and B together fill it in 10 hours. Find the time taken by B alone.
A. 23 hours
B. 20 hours
C. 30 hours
D. 16 hours
Answer: 20 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_find_pipe_rate_from_net_time
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{10}-\frac{1}{20}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
B=20
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{AT}{A-T}
\]
Answer: 20 hours

Q319. A job has 300 units. 12 workers finish 120 units, and 12 days are left. Find the extra workers required.

D. 2.25 workers
Answer: 3 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_contract_deadline_extra_workers
Explanation:

\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-N_c
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{180}{1\times 12}\right\rceil-12
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=3
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 3 workers

Q340. Pipe A fills a tank in 12 hours. Pipes A and B together fill it in 8 hours. Find the time taken by B alone.
A. 24 hours
B. 28 hours
C. 36 hours
D. 23 hours
Answer: 24 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_find_pipe_rate_from_net_time
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{8}-\frac{1}{12}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
B=24
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{AT}{A-T}
\]
Answer: 24 hours

\]

Q346. 10 workers have completed 80 units of a 300-unit job. Only 10 days remain. How many extra workers are needed?

D. 12 workers
Answer: 12 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_contract_deadline_extra_workers
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-N_c
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{220}{1\times 10}\right\rceil-10
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 12 workers

\]

W_A:W_B=\frac{1}{10}:\frac{1}{16}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
W_A:W_B=16:10
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
W_A:W_B=8:5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
E=8:5
\]
Answer: 8:5

---

Q378. A starts a job alone and works for 4 days. A and B then finish it together. A alone takes 20 days and B alone takes 30 days. Find the total time.
A. 18 3/5 days
B. 13 3/5 days
C. 10 1/5 days
D. 12 3/5 days
Answer: 13 3/5 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_delayed_join
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W_1=r_At_1,\ T_2=\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=6\times 4,\quad R=120-24
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=4+\frac{96}{6+4}=13.6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=4+\frac{120-6\times 4}{6+4}
\]
Answer: 13 3/5 days

Q416. A and B can finish a work alone in 30 and 8 days. A leaves 2 days before completion and B leaves 3 days before completion. Find the total time.
A. 7.28 days
B. 9.11 days
C. 8.11 days
D. 15.11 days
Answer: 9.11 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_backward_leave
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
r_A(T-a)+r_B(T-b)=W
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
28(T-2)+105(T-3)=840
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
T=9.11
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W+A\times 2+B\times 3}{A+B}
\]
Answer: 9.11 days

Q421. Pipe A fills a tank in 15 hours. Pipes A and B together fill it in 6 hours. Find the time taken by B alone.
A. 15 hours
B. 6 hours
C. 10 hours
D. 13 hours
Answer: 10 hours
Difficulty: Medium
Family: pc_find_pipe_rate_from_net_time
Explanation:
Write filling rates as positive and emptying rates as negative.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Find the effective tank rate.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{6}-\frac{1}{15}
\]
Divide the required tank work by that rate.
\[
B=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{AT}{A-T}
\]
Answer: 10 hours

\]

\]

Difficulty: Easy

Explanation:
Take the LCM as total work.
\[
W=LCM(15,21)=105
\]
Find each worker's one-period efficiency.
\[
A=105/15=7,\quad B=105/21=5
\]
Divide total work by the combined efficiency.
\[
T=\frac{105}{7+5}=8.75
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{15\times 21}{15+21}=8.75
\]
Answer: 8 3/4 days

---

Q453. 12 equal workers planned a job. After 8 days, 4 workers leave. Find how many days the work is delayed.
A. 10 1/2 days
B. 14 1/2 days
C. 6 4/5 days
D. 8 1/2 days
Answer: 8 1/2 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_worker_removed_delay
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
D=T_{actual}-T_{planned}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
R=300-12\times 8
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
D=\frac{204}{8}-\frac{204}{12}
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=T_a-T_p
\]
Answer: 8 1/2 days

Q454. A finishes in 30 days and B in 8 days. Find their efficiency ratio.

B. 30:8
C. 8:38

\[

\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
W_A:W_B=8:30
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
W_A:W_B=4:15
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
E=4:15
\]
Answer: 4:15

Q455. A starts a job alone and works for 5 days. A and B then finish it together. A alone takes 24 days and B alone takes 30 days. Find the total time.
A. 20.56 days
B. 16.56 days
C. 18 2/3 days
D. 15.56 days
Answer: 15.56 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_delayed_join
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W_1=r_At_1,\ T_2=\frac{W-W_1}{r_A+r_B}
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=5\times 5,\quad R=120-25
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=5+\frac{95}{5+4}=15.56
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=5+\frac{120-5\times 5}{5+4}
\]
Answer: 15.56 days

Q456. One team completes 15 units per day while another undoes 3 units per day. For 120 units, find the net completion time.
A. 16 days
B. 7 days
C. 8 days
D. 10 days
Answer: 10 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_digging_filling_negative_work
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_+-r_-}
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
T=\frac{120}{15-3}
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
T=10
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_n}
\]
Answer: 10 days

Q459. A two-hour work cycle gives 10 units in the first hour and 5 units in the second. Find the total hours for 72 units.

Answer: 9 2/5 hours
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_alternating_hours
Explanation:

\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]

\[
W_{cycle}=10+5
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=9.4
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 9 2/5 hours

Q462. A needs 30 days and B needs 24 days. In a joint job, A is absent for the last 2 days and B for the last 3 days. Find total duration.

C. 15.89 days
D. 13.89 days
Answer: 15.89 days

\[

\]

\[

\]

\[
T=15.89

\[

\]
Answer: 15.89 days

Q468. After a delay, 80 of 360 work units are done by 8 workers. To meet a 10-day deadline, find extra workers.

Explanation:

\[

\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{280}{1\times 10}\right\rceil-8
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=20
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 20 workers

Answer: 5:8

Q490. One team completes 12 units per day while another undoes 4 units per day. For 150 units, find the net completion time.
A. 18 3/4 days
B. 15 3/4 days
C. 22 1/2 days
D. 14 3/4 days
Answer: 18 3/4 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_digging_filling_negative_work
Explanation:
Write output as rate multiplied by active time.
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_+-r_-}
\]
Add the outputs of all active agents.
\[
T=\frac{150}{12-4}
\]
Simplify the total output.
\[
T=18.75
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=\frac{W}{r_n}
\]
Answer: 18 3/4 days

Q491. A alone needs 12 days, while A and B together need 10 days. How many days will B alone need?
A. 60 days
B. 2 days
C. 22 days
D. 11 days
Answer: 60 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_individual_from_combined
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{T}-\frac{1}{A}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
\frac{1}{B}=\frac{1}{10}-\frac{1}{12}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
B=60
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
B=\frac{10\times 12}{12-10}=60
\]
Answer: 60 days

Q492. The work cycle has three turns with outputs 8, 5, 3 units. The total work is 120 units. Find the completion time.
A. 20 days
B. 22 days
C. 33 days
D. 18 days
Answer: 22 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_alternating_group_cycle
Explanation:
Find the work completed in one full cycle.
\[
W_{cycle}=\sum r_i
\]
Count full cycles before the last turn.
\[
W_{cycle}=8+5+3
\]
Add only the needed part of the final turn.
\[
T=22
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=cycles+final\ turn
\]
Answer: 22 days

Q493. 720 person-days of food are available. 30 people consume it for 8 days, then the group becomes 40. Find how many more days the food will last.
A. 15 days
B. 14 2/5 days
C. 17 days
D. 12 days
Answer: 12 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_food_remaining_stock
Explanation:
Convert the stock into consumption units.
\[
D=\frac{S-consumed}{people}
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
D=\frac{720-30\times 8}{40}
\]
Divide the remaining stock by the new daily use.
\[
D=12
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
D=\frac{S_l}{P_2}
\]
Answer: 12 days

Q494. If a job takes 18 days and 5 days have already been worked, what part is still unfinished?
A. 18/13
B. 13/18
C. 5/18
D. 13/5
Answer: 13/18
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_basic_residual_work
Explanation:
Set up the rate relation for this case.
\[
R=1-\frac{t}{T}
\]
Put the given values into that relation.
\[
R=1-\frac{5}{18}
\]
Solve for the asked value.
\[
R=\frac{13}{18}=13/18
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
R=\frac{18-5}{18}=13/18
\]
Answer: 13/18

Q495. A job changes team after 4 days. A, B and C alone take 8, 12 and 10 days. If B and C join, find the completion time.
A. 1.62 days
B. 6 3/4 days
C. 4.62 days
D. 5.62 days
Answer: 5.62 days
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_multi_phase_join_leave
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
\sum r_it_i=W
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=15\times 4
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=4+\frac{60}{37}=5.62
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=t_1+\frac{W-W_1}{r_2}
\]
Answer: 5.62 days

Q497. A stock is enough for 30 people for 24 days. Find its duration for 40 people.
A. 15 days
B. 18 days
C. 27 days
D. 22 days
Answer: 18 days
Difficulty: Easy
Family: tw_food_resource_basic
Explanation:

\[
M_1D_1=M_2D_2
\]
Subtract the stock already consumed.
\[
30\times 24=40\times D_2
\]

\]

Q498. After a delay, 80 of 240 work units are done by 8 workers. To meet a 12-day deadline, find extra workers.
A. 12 workers
B. 9 workers
C. 7.5 workers
D. 6 workers
Answer: 6 workers
Difficulty: Hard
Family: tw_contract_deadline_extra_workers
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{R}{r\times t}\right\rceil-N_c
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
N_{extra}=\left\lceil\frac{160}{1\times 12}\right\rceil-8
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
N_{extra}=6
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
N_{extra}=N_r-N_c
\]
Answer: 6 workers

Q500. A and B start a work together. A alone takes 24 days and B alone takes 6 days. A leaves after 4 days; B finishes the rest. Find the total time.
A. 5 days
B. 1 day
C. 6 days
D. 2 days
Answer: 5 days
Difficulty: Medium
Family: tw_forward_leave
Explanation:
Split the work into timeline phases.
\[
W=(r_A+r_B)t_1+r_Bt_2
\]
Subtract the work already completed.
\[
W_1=(5+20)\times 4
\]
Use the active rate for the remaining phase.
\[
T=4+\frac{20}{20}=5
\]
Shortcut / Exam Method:
\[
T=4+\frac{W-W_1}{B}
\]
Answer: 5 days
